The answer is: 1, 2, and 3  Explanation: The text corresponding to the speech is a dialogue between two characters, SURELY and SHE, who are having a conversation. The speaker (SURELY) is expressing their frustration and excitement, while the other speaker (SHE) is responding in a similar way. This is an example of parallel structure, where the two speakers use similar phrases and sentences to convey their thoughts and feelings.
